| # Phase 13 confirmatory campaign: C13.1 primary (A-vs-C AND A-vs-D),
| # with the B/D/S ablation ladder and the C13.2 corruption sweep.
| #
| # ============================================================
| # PRE-REGISTRATION. Committed before the campaign was run.
| # The companion document is experiments/phase13-social-preregistration.md;
| # every decision rule, constant, epoch, SESOI, and claim ceiling is locked
| # there, from the Stage B pilot (experiments/phase13-social-pilot.campaign,
| # disjoint seeds 13901..13908).
| # ============================================================
| #
| # EXPECTED OUTCOME, STATED IN ADVANCE: null on C13.1. The ladder's levers
| # (D-120) have not yet produced what transmission needs; the value here is
| # the controlled why - B, C, D, S and the F-curve say which link fails -
| # plus the artifacts every later lever reuses. The pilot measured
| # reachability decisively non-null (hearers and speakers in the hundreds
| # to thousands per world), so a null will not be a reachability null
| # unless this campaign's own censuses say otherwise, world by world.
| #
| # Arms differ from A in exactly their named variables (D-118's lesson);
| # observational is common-mode in A/B/C/D; the sweep arms are A at three
| # corruption levels for C13.2's F-curve.
|
